Ottoman war ending (Konya falling), reducing Defense 90→70 and CityDefense 85→60. Maximizing Offense 100, Mobilization 100, Air 100, Nuke 100, UseNuke 100 for total war. Increasing Naval 75→85 for amphibious assault capabilities against Dutch coastal cities. Decolonization proposal by Brazil is only hope to reset city-state influence - must prepare for war if that fails.

- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Bismarck, leader of Germany (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Germany
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Landsknecht, Replacing Tercio (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Unit: Krupp Gun, Replacing Artillery (Requires technology: Replaceable Parts)
        - Unique Building: Hanse, Replacing Customs House (Requires technology: Guilds)
        - Unique Building: Brewhouse, Replacing Windmill (Requires technology: Economics)
        - Civilization Ability: Gain +1 Influence per turn with City-States for each gifted Unit (lost when killed). -15% Production and Gold costs for Military Units.
      - Leader: Bismarck
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      
      ## Policies
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Military-Industrial Complex (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Military-Industrial Complex: -33% Gold cost of purchasing units. +3 Science from Defense Buildings, Citadels, Forts, and Unique Improvements.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 9
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 8
        - WarBias: 8
        - HostileBias: 10
        - WarmongerHate: 8

        - GuardedBias: 7
        - AfraidBias: 5

        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 8
        - Loyalty: 8
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 3
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 3
        - MinorCivWarBias: 3
        - DenounceWillingness: 8
        - Forgiveness: 1
        - Meanness: 10
        - Neediness: 5
        - Chattiness: 4
        - DeceptiveBias: 3
        - Rationale: DUAL NUCLEAR THREAT RESPONSE: Portugal's Manhattan Project completion creates second nuclear threat alongside Netherlands. Maximum HostileBias (10) maintained for inevitable confrontation with Freedom-aligned nuclear powers. Forgiveness at 1 - Netherlands' betrayal of Mogadishu and Portugal's nuclear arming are unforgivable. Meanness at 10 for maximum pressure. MinorCivFriendlyBias at 10 to preserve remaining city-state allies from Dutch/Portuguese influence warfare.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Radar
        - Rationale: RADAR PRIORITY CONFIRMED: 7 turns to Nuclear Fission, then immediate Radar for Fighter production. Dutch and Portuguese bomber fleets are existential threat - without Fighters my cities are defenseless against nuclear delivery systems. Radar also enables Advanced Ballistics path if Nuclear Missiles needed for retaliation. No alternative technologies acceptable at this stage.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Elite Forces (Policy)
        - Rationale: ELITE FORCES REMAINS CRITICAL: +15 XP for new units and 50% more combat experience essential for nuclear age warfare. With 17 turns to next policy, this will accelerate unit quality for impending Dutch war. Military-Industrial Complex tempting for gold savings, but unit quality takes priority when facing numerically superior Freedom alliance. Alternative tenets less valuable than immediate combat effectiveness.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Assyria
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 15
        - Rationale: ASSYRIA MAINTENANCE: Assyria remains Rome's vassal fighting Brazil/Ottomans. Shared Autocracy ideology (+999) ensures natural alignment. Weak military (1,088) makes them non-threat but useful distraction. Private warming (15) maintains cooperation without binding commitments that Rome already provides. No change needed.
        - UpdatedTurn: 389
        
        ### Rome
        - Public: 85
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: ROME ALLIANCE CRITICAL: Rome is sole reliable Autocracy ally with shared ideology, Defensive Pact intact, coordinated opposition to Brazil/Ottomans. They provide 7 delegates and have vassalized Assyria. Essential anchor for diplomatic/military coordination against Netherlands nuclear threat. Their war against Brazil prevents Brazilian cultural victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### The Netherlands
        - Public: -95
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: MAINTAIN MAXIMUM NETHERLANDS HOSTILITY: Netherlands continues attacking Mogadishu (my protected city-state, influence 398) despite our DoF - active nuclear-age betrayal. They possess Manhattan Project + 5 Uranium. Public -95 signals treachery to world; Private -100 ensures no hesitation when atomic bombs ready. Once Nuclear Fission completes, Dutch cities face fire for this unforgivable treachery against protected ally.
        - UpdatedTurn: 397
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: -35
        - Private: -40
        - Rationale: OTTOMAN WAR MAINTAINED: Continue moderate hostility adjustment. Ottoman military weakened by multi-front war (vs me, Rome, Assyria, Netherlands, America, plus city-states). War weariness 17% for them vs my 16%. They lost Tank, Light Tank, Infantry units this turn. Konya under pressure. Maintain containment while preserving strength for Netherlands/Portugal nuclear confrontation.
        - UpdatedTurn: 401
        
        ### America
        - Public: -45
        - Private: -70
        - Rationale: AMERICAN BETRAYAL CERTAINTY: America remains Netherlands' vassal state with Declaration of Friendship to both parties. When nuclear war erupts against Netherlands (10 turns), Washington will be forced to attack per vassalage obligations. Current moderate hostility (-35/-55) insufficient for imminent betrayal scenario. Escalating to (-45/-70) ensures tactical AI prepares for American entry into war alongside Dutch nuclear aggression. No diplomatic trust possible with puppet state of primary adversary.
        - UpdatedTurn: 404
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: -30
        - Private: -40
        - Rationale: BRAZIL HOSTILITY FURTHER REDUCED: Brazil continues fighting Netherlands (my primary nuclear threat), effectively bleeding Dutch strength. With Konya falling, Brazil's war against Netherlands becomes even more valuable. Reduced from -50/-60 to -30/-40 signals potential for tacit coordination against Dutch Freedom alliance. Brazil still Order ideology competitor, but their survival keeps Netherlands distracted from atomic buildup.
        - UpdatedTurn: 407
        
        ### Portugal
        - Public: -75
        - Private: -90
        - Rationale: PORTUGAL HOSTILITY INCREASED: Portugal's continued naval buildup (second Battleship produced Turn 406) with Manhattan Project + 4 Uranium creates severe dual nuclear threat. They just traded with Rome for resources, potentially preparing for war. Escalating to -75/-90 ensures tactical AI prepares for preventive action if Portuguese nuclear weapons threaten German cities. Cannot allow Lisbon to become second nuclear launch platform.
        - UpdatedTurn: 407
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: NUCLEAR TRANSITION PHASE: Ottoman war ending (Konya collapses next turn), pivoting to Dutch/Portuguese nuclear confrontation. Reduced Defense from 100→90 and CityDefense 100→85 as imminent Ottoman threat diminishes. Increased Offense 40→50 for transition to offensive operations against nuclear threats. Maintained Nuke 100/UseNuke 95/Antiair 100/Air 95 for atomic age warfare. Netherlands at 41/45 delegates - potential diplomatic victory in 4 turns. Need Radar→Fighters urgently for bomber defense. Science 100 and Production 100 critical for atomic arsenal buildup post-Fission. Espionage 100 to protect science cities and spy on Dutch/Portuguese nuclear programs. Spaceship remains long-term goal but secondary to surviving nuclear age.
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 45
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 3 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Open Door: Mombasa:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 375
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Jakarta:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: 375
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Panama City:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Mogadishu:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 397
          - World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: 397
          - Sanction: Assyria:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Lutetia:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - Proposer: Portugal
          - Enact: Decolonization:
            - Description: Enforce global decolonization. If successful, the Influence of all Players with all City-States is lowered to 50, unless they are permanent allies.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: Brazil
          - Enact: International Games:
            - Description: Begins the International Games project. Once underway, Civilizations can contribute Production towards its completion by selecting it in the city production list. When complete, Civilizations receive bonuses based on how much they contributed.
              : Olympic Village wonder appears in Capital. (Receive +3 Happiness and +3 Culture in the City in which it is built. 25% of the Culture from World Wonders and Tiles is added to the Tourism output of the City. All Stadiums receive +2 Gold and Tourism.)
              : Receive a free Great Person of your choice.
              : Tourism increases by 50% for 20 Turns.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: The Netherlands

        You, Bismarck (leader of Germany, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 407.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Bismarck, leader of Germany (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Germany
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Landsknecht, Replacing Tercio (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Unit: Krupp Gun, Replacing Artillery (Requires technology: Replaceable Parts)
        - Unique Building: Hanse, Replacing Customs House (Requires technology: Guilds)
        - Unique Building: Brewhouse, Replacing Windmill (Requires technology: Economics)
        - Civilization Ability: Gain +1 Influence per turn with City-States for each gifted Unit (lost when killed). -15% Production and Gold costs for Military Units.
      - Leader: Bismarck
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      
      ## Policies
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Military-Industrial Complex (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Military-Industrial Complex: -33% Gold cost of purchasing units. +3 Science from Defense Buildings, Citadels, Forts, and Unique Improvements.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Computers
        - Rationale: Researching Computers next will allow us to unlock Satellites, which is crucial for building the Hubble Space Telescope, mapping the world, and progressing towards late-game spaceship components for our science victory.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Adopting the Military-Industrial Complex tenet will provide a massive science boost from our numerous defense buildings, forts, and citadels, directly accelerating our progress toward a science victory while also lowering gold costs for military units.
        - Policy: Military-Industrial Complex
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
        - Flavors:
          - Production: 100
          - Nuke: 100
          - UseNuke: 95
          - Culture: 50
          - Espionage: 100
          - Spaceship: 100
          - Science: 100
      - rationale: We maintain our Spaceship grand strategy while raising our Culture flavor to 50. This encourages the construction of Broadcast Towers to complete the Hanoi city-state quest, speeds up policy adoption, and bolsters our cultural defense against the Netherlands' imminent cultural victory threat.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 62668
    - reasoningTokens: 8850
    - outputTokens: 220
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Current Strategic Landscape**
            
            I'm currently assessing our position within the Atomic Era, specifically on Prince difficulty with Vox Populi. My focus is on understanding our existing empire's strengths and weaknesses in relation to the global stage to inform our next strategic moves.
            
            **Evaluating Technological Advancements**
            
            My current focus is on our technology trajectory towards a Science Victory. With Nuclear Fission nearing completion, I'm reviewing available paths like Radar, Electronics, and Computers, while also considering the strategic implications of our current policy, Elite Forces, and our standing with global powers.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Threats**
            
            I'm currently evaluating the precarious state of global alliances. While I'm at war with the Ottomans and have friendly ties with Rome, Assyria, and America, the Netherlands presents a significant threat. Their rapid cultural dominance and strong influence over key nations demand immediate attention and a shift in our diplomatic and military strategies.
            
            **Prioritizing Immediate Threats**
            
            I'm realizing the Netherlands is alarmingly close to a Cultural Victory, potentially within 10-15 turns, and they also hold significant sway in Diplomatic victories. My attention is now fully on this immediate crisis, and assessing our current war with the Ottomans to see if it can be leveraged.
            
            **Conquering Ottomans**
            
            I'm currently focused on our offensive against the Ottomans, pushing hard into Konya with superior forces. This expansion strengthens our position, but the looming threat of the Netherlands' imminent Cultural and Diplomatic Victory demands my immediate attention.
            
            **Leveraging Diplomatic Opportunities**
            
            I've discovered the Netherlands is now at war with Brazil and the Ottomans, disrupting their trade and tourism, which is excellent. I'm focusing on the upcoming World Congress, aiming to vote for Brazil's "Decolonization" proposal to weaken the Netherlands' city-state influence, and will adjust our diplomatic strategy accordingly.
            
            **Adapting Diplomatic Settings**
            
            I'm examining our current persona settings to see how they align with the evolving diplomatic landscape. Given the Netherlands' aggressive cultural push and our ongoing conflicts, I believe a slight adjustment to certain traits might be beneficial for navigating the upcoming World Congress and influencing other civs' behaviors without overplaying our hand.
            
            **Adjusting Persona Parameters**
            
            I'm currently reviewing our persona settings. Given the Netherlands' imminent victory, I'm considering increasing `VictoryCompetitiveness` to 10. I'm also examining our public and private diplomatic modifiers, especially our relationship with the Netherlands, to strategize effectively.
            
            **Countering Global Influence**
            
            I'm now focusing on how to disrupt the Netherlands' global influence. While I've already maxed out my hostility towards them, I'm investigating ways to improve relations with nations actively opposing them, like Brazil, to build a united front. Rome's friendship with the Netherlands is a complication I'm actively analyzing.
            
            **Revising Strategic Objectives**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our diplomatic standing with Rome, ensuring we maintain a strong relationship despite other ongoing conflicts. My focus has shifted to the cascading implications of the Ottoman situation on the Netherlands' victory conditions, recognizing that their elimination could accelerate their cultural dominance by reducing the civ count needed for victory.
            
            **Analyzing Victory Conditions**
            
            I'm recalculating the Cultural Victory threshold. With the potential for civ elimination, the number needed could drop significantly, and I've confirmed that influencing all *remaining* civs is the condition. This means if the Ottomans fall, the target number decreases, and the Netherlands could win with just one more ally.
            
            **Assessing Victory Thresholds**
            
            I'm analyzing how the elimination of the Ottomans impacts the Cultural Victory condition for the Netherlands. If the Ottomans fall, they'd only need to influence Brazil, which is already a significant concern. I'm weighing the benefit of capturing Ottoman cities against accelerating the Netherlands' win.
            
            **Researching Key Technologies**
            
            I'm currently analyzing our technological path, recognizing our spaceship victory goal. With Nuclear Fission nearing completion, I'm prioritizing research into Radar, Electronics, and Computers, weighing their immediate tactical and strategic benefits against our long-term spaceship objectives and current policy choices.
            
            **Revising Tech Priorities**
            
            I'm reassessing our technology path. It seems my understanding of bomber unit unlocks was slightly off; regular bombers are available earlier. My focus is now firmly on Computers for Satellites, as it's critical for our Science Victory goals, with Electronics also being a key stepping stone.
            
            **Choosing Next Technology**
            
            My focus has shifted to selecting our next technological research. Given the critical importance of unlocking Satellites for our Science Victory and the Hubble Space Telescope, I've decided to prioritize `Computers`. This choice aligns with our long-term goals and the path to our spaceship victory.
            
            **Selecting Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our Autocracy tenets. While `Elite Forces` boosts military experience, `Military-Industrial Complex` offers a significant science boost from defense buildings and citadels, which is crucial for my Science Victory goals and our wide empire. I'm leaning towards `Military-Industrial Complex`.
            
            **Selecting Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm reassessing our Autocracy tenets. While `Elite Forces` is selected, `Military-Industrial Complex` and `New World Order` offer strong benefits for science and production respectively. However, `Rights of Man` from Rationalism provides a substantial science boost during Golden Ages, which aligns well with our current situation and victory goals.
            
            **Reviewing Policy Tenets**
            
            I'm reviewing our policy selections across all branches. It seems I've spread our tenets too thinly. I need to consolidate within Autocracy to unlock higher-tier benefits and better support our Science Victory, particularly by focusing on the synergy between tenets.
            
            **Selecting Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm refining our Autocracy tenet choices. We currently have two Tier 1 tenets and one Tier 2 tenet. To unlock another Tier 2 tenet, we only need to select one more Tier 1, as the requirement for a Tier 2 is two Tier 1 tenets.
            
            **Selecting Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm analyzing our Autocracy tenets again. I initially thought we needed another Tier 1 to unlock a Tier 2, but I'm realizing we already have two Tier 1 tenets and one Tier 2. It seems I was mistaken about needing more Tier 1 tenets to unlock a second Tier 2; the system likely only shows available options.
            
            **Choosing Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm reconsidering our Autocracy tenets to maximize science output for our victory goals. Given our numerous defense buildings and citadels, `Military-Industrial Complex` is proving to be an exceptionally strong choice, providing a significant science boost that directly supports our Science Victory path.
            
            **Choosing Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm reassessing our Autocracy tenets again. I've decided `Military-Industrial Complex` is the optimal choice, as the science boost from defensive buildings and citadels will significantly accelerate our Science Victory. The reduction in unit purchase cost is a secondary, but welcome, benefit.
            
            **Selecting Autocracy Tenet**
            
            I've decided to select "Military-Industrial Complex" as my next Autocracy tenet. This choice offers a valuable science boost from defensive structures, directly aiding my Science Victory pursuit, and also provides a welcome reduction in unit costs.
            
            **Analyzing Ottoman Weakness**
            
            I'm currently assessing our war with the Ottomans. Their low happiness and struggling military present a clear opportunity. I'm focused on concluding this conflict swiftly and efficiently, as their continued existence might inadvertently benefit the Netherlands by potentially slowing their dominance and providing a distraction.
            
            **Adjusting Culture Flavors**
            
            I'm realizing our culture flavor is critically low at 10, which is detrimental to countering the Netherlands' cultural victory push. I need to significantly boost our culture investment to build up our defenses and hinder their progress.
            
            **Boosting Culture Defenses**
            
            My current focus is on dramatically increasing our culture output. With the Netherlands' tourism already influential, I need to raise our culture defense to push back their influence and gain policies faster, which is critical for progressing through Autocracy tenets.
            
            **Shifting Espionage Focus**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our espionage targets. With the Netherlands being our primary threat, I'm considering redirecting spies from the Ottomans to gather intelligence on them, aiming to disrupt their victory progress and potentially steal key technologies. This shift is critical to countering their global influence.
            
            **Analyzing Tech Parity**
            
            I'm reassessing our technological standing; the Netherlands is now slightly ahead in total techs researched. I'm also reviewing our diplomatic relationships, noting our highly hostile stance with the Netherlands and our beneficial pact with Rome, while also considering Brazil's guarded but potentially valuable opposition to the Netherlands.
            
            **Improving Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm currently analyzing our relationship with Brazil. Despite denouncing them earlier, their ongoing war with the Netherlands presents an opportunity. I'm exploring ways to mend fences and potentially form an alliance against our common enemy, while also navigating Rome's complex relationship with both us and the Netherlands.
            
            **Adjusting Culture Output**
            
            I'm focusing on significantly increasing our culture output to 50. This will bolster our defenses against the Netherlands' cultural victory push, help complete a city-state quest, and allow us to acquire more Autocracy tenets faster. Our Grand Strategy remains `Spaceship`, and existing relationships are optimized.
            
            **Refining Flavor Settings**
            
            I'm adjusting our culture flavor to 50, which will boost our defenses against the Netherlands' cultural dominance. This also helps with the Hanoi quest for broadcast towers and allows faster acquisition of Autocracy tenets. My focus remains on a Spaceship victory.
            
            **Selecting Next Tech**
            
            My current focus is on selecting our next research, and I've confirmed that `Computers` is the optimal choice, as it directly leads to Satellites and is crucial for our Science Victory. I've also verified the exact spelling for `Military-Industrial Complex` and our `Spaceship` grand strategy.
            
            **Finalizing Tenet and Flavors**
            
            I've decided to lock in "Military-Industrial Complex" for its substantial science boost and unit cost reduction. My focus now is on finalizing the flavor settings, ensuring our Culture output is at 50 to counter the Netherlands and accelerate policy acquisition, all while maintaining our Spaceship victory goal.
